🤖 RAG ANSWER (47.7s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   To apply for the Erasmus internship program, follow these steps:  1.
   **Check eligibility**: Undergraduate students must have a minimum GPA of
   2.20, and graduate students a GPA of 2.5. The internship must be in a
   field related to your degree program and last at least 2 months.  2.
   **Submit required documents**: Apply via the Online Application System by
   the deadline (announced in October) with a CV, transcript, and an
   acceptance letter from the host organization (minimum 2-month internship).
   3. **Selection process**: Applicants are ranked based on their Erasmus+
   score (considering GPA and language scores). A student list is approved by
   the Selection Committee, and grant allocations are assigned sequentially.
   4. **Finalize agreement**: After approval, sign the Erasmus+ Internship
   Mobility Agreement with the host organization before starting the
   internship.  5. **Additional criteria**: Students can participate in
   Erasmus+ programs for up to 12 months per study cycle
   (undergraduate/master’s/doctoral). Final-year students may apply if they
   meet the academic calendar requirements.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (53.5s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Erasmus öğrenim hareketliliğinde hibe hesaplaması şu şekilde yapılır:  1.
   **Erasmus+ Puanı**: Öğrencinin GNO'sunun %50'si ve yabancı dil sınav
   puanının (TOEFL/YDS/ELAE) %50'si alınarak hesaplanır.  2. **Sıralama ve
   Hibeler**: En yüksek Erasmus+ Puanına sahip öğrenciler Kariyer Geliştirme
   ve Staj Ofisi tarafından listelenir. Bu liste Öğrenci Seçim Komitesi
   onayından geçirilir.  3. **Hibe Miktarı**: Hibeler, yurt dışındaki eğitim
   süresiyle orantılıdır. Ulusal Ajans her yıl ülke bazında aylık hibe
   miktarlarını ve ödeme şekillerini belirler. Toplam hibe, aylık hibe
   miktarı ile hareketlilik süresi (ay ve gün bazında) çarpı

================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (74.2s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The Learning Agreement (LA) process for Sabancı University exchange
   students involves several key steps. Students prepare, sign, and submit
   the LA within the timeline set by the International Relations Office
   (IRO), specifying selected courses, equivalent SU credits, course types,
   and ECTS values, with approval from their exchange academic advisor. The
   advisor reviews and approves course selections, credit equivalencies, and
   ECTS values, or requests changes if needed. For Erasmus+ grants, the first
   installment requires a signed LA and Grant Agreement. Graduate students
   preparing for short-term mobility must detail their research/project in
   the LA and handle visa, insurance, and accommodation independently. After
   completing the LA, students submit it to the partner institution for
   signing and then to IRO. Upon return, they must provide the signed LA,
   partner university transcript, and a stamped Certificate of Attendance to
   IRO. Students are responsible for ensuring their course selections align
   with SU graduation requirements and informing their advisor of any
   schedule changes before the partner institution’s term ends.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (46.0s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   A student has 15 days to object to a disciplinary sanction to the
   University Administrative Board. The University concludes the objection
   within 15 days. If the objection is rejected, the disciplinary board or
   supervisor reviews the decision considering the rejection grounds. If a
   student chooses to appeal to the administrative jurisdiction, and a stay
   of execution is granted, the suspension of the disciplinary punishment is
   notified in writing within one day of receiving the court order.
   Notifications to relevant units are made within three days after the
   objection is concluded. Students can also object to dormitory-related
   punishments, with objections to removal decisions decided within 15
   working days by the Provincial Directorate. Objections to warnings or
   reprimands must be submitted within five working days.
------------------------------------------------------------

   yargı hükmüne ihtiyaç duyulan durumlarda, zaman aşımı süresi bu hükmün
   kesinleştiği günden başlar. Cinsel taciz/saldırı gibi suçlar için de,
   eylemin işlendiği tarihten itibaren en geç 2 yıl içinde soruşturma
   tamamlanmalı ve karara bağlanmalıdır. Disiplin Kurulu veya Disiplin Amiri,
   karar verme süresi olarak 10 gün tanınır; bu süre, soruşturmanın
   tamamlandığı günden itibaren başlar.

   provide financial support to successful undergraduate and graduate
   students in need. It is expanded through donations from SU employees,
   alumni, students, and other individuals or institutions. Donations from
   employees are processed via salary deductions, with HR managing the
   contributions and IPAR overseeing fund development. The fund’s revenues
   include 90% of interest income. International donations are facilitated
   through the "Friends of Sabancı University Fund" under the Turkish
   Philanthropic Fund in New York. The fund’s management principles and
   donation procedures are governed by institutional guidelines, with the
   Board of Trustees approving key policies.

   arasında başvuru yapar ve mülakat aşamasında "Çalışma Burslu Öğrenci Alım
   Değerlendirme Formu" doldurulur. Seçim, başvuru sahiplerinin tanıtım
   etkinliklerine önceki katılımları, yararlılığı, referansları ve "Çalışma
   Burslu Öğrenci Seçimi Talimatı"na göre yapılır. Başvurular, Hababam
   sisteminde "Burs ve Mali Destek Programı" sekmesi üzerinden incelenir. İş
   ilanı, SUform sistinde "Job announcement" formu ile verilir ve öğrenciler
   bu formu doldurarak olumlu yanıt verir. Seçilen öğrencilere sonuçlar
   duyurulur. İşe alınan öğrenciler, bir dönemlik sözleşme imzalar ve bu
   sözleşme dönem başlarında yenilenebilir. Öğrencilerin Genel Not Ortalaması
   (GNO) en az 3.50 ve destekledikleri dersten en az A- almış olmaları
   gerekir. Ayrıca, iki aşamalı oryantasyon programına katılmaları beklenir:
   ilk aşamada sosyal iletişim ve davranış, ikinci aşamada üniversitenin
   teknik bilgileri anlatılır.

   Lisansüstü programlarda tekrarlanan derslerde GNO hesaplamasında,
   öğrencinin daha önce almış olduğu dersin puanı GNO hesaplamalarından
   düşülür ve en son alınan dersin notu dikkate alınır. GNO hesaplamalarında,
   tekrar edilen derslerden alınan en son not kullanılır. Elde edilen GNO,

   değerlendirmelerde tüm ondalık haneler hesaplama sürecinde dikkate alınır.

   hesaplamasına dahil edilir. Notu B veya B’den yüksek olan dersler
   tekrarlanamaz.

[Generation] Stripped document codes from answer.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (50.5s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The graduate program opening process at Sabancı University requires the
   Graduate School Board to submit a proposal with program details (courses,
   credits, purpose) to the Academic Council (AC) after obtaining the
   Undergraduate Education Council (UEC) opinion under the Vice President for
   Education (VP-E) coordination. The AC may approve, request additional
   information, or reject the proposal. If approved, the proposal proceeds to
   the Board of Trustees for final approval, followed by notification to the
   Council of Higher Education (YOK). For closure, the Graduate School Board
   initiates the process with UEC input, and the AC reviews the proposal
   before submitting it to the Board of Trustees. After YOK approval, the
   Student Records (SR) unit updates YOKSIS and archives program data to
   preserve student history. Changes to existing programs (e.g., course
   requirements) follow the Procedure for Opening/Closing Courses. Doctorate
   program openings follow a similar structure, with the Graduate School
   Board responsible for submitting required information to YOKSIS.
------------------------------------------------------------

📊 VERIFIED FACTS (From Knowledge Graph):

[Gpa Calculation]
  • Requirement: alınan tüm dersler GNO hesaplarında kullanılır (lisans)
  • Requirement: all digits after comma are taken into consideration
  • Requirement: last grade for a repeated course is taken into account
  • Requirement: tekrar edilen derslerden alınan en son not dikkate alınır (lisans)
  • Credits: total SU credits of the courses taken in that term (lisansustu)

🔗 RELATIONS (From Knowledge Graph):

  • Sabancı University Grad...
================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (61.8s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Sabancı University calculates GPA (Grade Point Average) by dividing the
   total SU credit points earned in a term by the total SU credits of the
   courses taken in that term. For cumulative GPA (CGPA), the calculation
   includes all courses taken since enrollment. Undergraduate students use
   all courses taken, with the last grade of repeated courses counted, and
   results are displayed with two decimal places, though all decimal digits
   are considered in academic rankings. Graduate programs follow the same
   method, with CGPA calculated using all courses since enrollment, and
   repeated courses’ final grades included. Both undergraduate and graduate
   GPAs require SU credit-based calculations.
------------------------------------------------------------

   listeleri fakültelere/enstitülere iletir. Fakülteler/enstitüler, bu
   listeleri inceleyerek gerekli düzeltmeleri yapar ve Dekan/Enstitü Müdürü
   onayı ile ÖK’ya geri gönderir. Mezuniyet başvurusu yapmak için öğrencinin
   ilgili dönemde ders kaydı yapılması veya değişim programı kapsamında
   öğrenim görmesi gerekir. Başvuru son gününü izleyen 4 hafta içinde,
   başvurusu kabul edilmeyen öğrencilerin başvuruları reddedilir ve e-posta

  • faculty member → [responsible for] → pr...
================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (57.9s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The Leave of Absence process for students at Sabancı University involves
   submitting a request with documented reasons (health, financial, family,
   personal, academic, or unexpected situations). Undergraduate students must
   apply by the last business day of the **4th week** following the academic
   year they registered, paying **25%** of the semester fee for a semester
   leave or **25%** of the annual tuition for an academic year leave.
   Graduate students, if applying due to conscription, arrest, or similar
   legal situations, must do so within **2 months** after the situation
   arises, with approval from the Graduate School Administrative Board. For
   all students, leave requests require proof of the stated reason, and
   unpaid leave suspends university benefits. Failure to return within **3
   business days** without notice after unpaid leave results in contract
   termination. Returning students must apply through the Graduate School
   Directorate, and any paid leave fees are deducted from the tuition of the
   semester they return to.
------------------------------------------------------------

   Diploma programı değişikliği için öğrenci, önce akademik danışmanına
   başvurarak görüş almalıdır. Akademik danışman, öğrenciyi program
   değişikliği süreci ve ilgili diploma programı hakkında bilgilendirir,
   gerekirse ilgili alan danışmanlarına yönlendirir. Başvurular, akademik

   isteyen öğrencinin, dilekçe ve formu ile geçiş yapmak istediği programın

   ile yapılır. Başvurular, ilgili fakülte tarafından incelenir ve Fakülte
   Yönetim Kurulu kararı ile sonuçlandırılır. Sonuçlar, ders kayıtları
   başlamadan önce öğrencilere duyurulur. Program değişikliği başvuruları,
   başvuruların başlamasından iki hafta önce başlamalıdır.

   borrow up to 60 books for a period of 60 days. The due dates for these
   books can be renewed unlimitedly for the same period, provided no other
   user has requested the item. This borrowing limit applies specifically to
   the "General Book Collection" and "Large-Size Book Collection," as
   students are restricted from accessing the "Faculty Reserve Collection"
   and "Student Reserve Collection."

🤖 RAG ANSWER (79.3s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The disciplinary penalties for students at Sabancı University include
   warnings, reprimands, temporary suspensions (ranging from one week to one
   month or one to two semesters), and dismissal. For warnings, reprimands,
   and short suspensions (up to one month), disciplinary proceedings must be
   initiated within **1 month** of the offense being reported; for
   suspensions of one or two semesters or dismissal, proceedings must start
   within **3 months**. Students receiving suspension (one week to one month
   or one to two semesters) are required to pay the semester tuition they are
   responsible for, and their suspension period is **not counted** toward the
   maximum study period. Repeated disciplinary infractions result in a
   **heavier penalty** (one grade higher), but dismissal cannot be imposed
   for a repeated offense. Dormitory-specific penalties (e.g., removal from
   housing) follow separate procedures, with appeals to the Disciplinary
   Board allowed within **5 working days** of notification, and decisions on
   objections made within **7 working days**. Notifications of penalties are
   sent to the student, scholarship/loan providers, and relevant university
   units, with dormitory removal cases also evaluated under the Procedure on
   Leaving the Student Residences.
------------------------------------------------------------

================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (83.0s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Bir dersin tekrar edilebilirliği öğrenci statüsüne ve alınan notuna
   bağlıdır. **Lisans öğrencileri** için, daha önce geçer not (örneğin "F",
   "U", "NA" veya "W") almış oldukları dersleri, aldıkları dönemi izleyen en
   çok üç dönem (izinli dönemler ve yaz dönemleri hariç) içinde
   tekrarlayabilirler. **Lisansüstü öğrencileri** ise "C", "C+" veya "B-"
   notu almış oldukları dersleri, programdaki dersleri tamamlama süresinin
   sonuna kadar tekrar alabilir. Ancak lisansüstü öğrencilerin "B", "A-" veya
   "A" notu almış oldukları dersleri tekrar almasına izin verilmez. Zorunlu
   derslerin tekrarı mezuniyete kadar gerekli olurken, seçmeli derslerin
   tekrarı veya ders saydırma (yerine başka bir ders almak) danışman onayıyla
   mümkündür. Tekrarlanan derslerde, önceki dersin puanı GNO hesaplarından
   düşülür ve son dersin puanı hesaplama sürecine dahil edilir.

   yapılmazsa fiyat teklifinin yarısı, saatten sonra iptal edilirse tamamı
   faturaya eklenir**.  6. Öğrenci talepleri, ilgili birimden gelmesi ve
   onaylanması durumunda değerlendirilir.   Araç uygun olmazsa alternatif
   ulaşım planlanmalıdır.

   the application period via the academic calendar. Students apply through

   compliance with conditions like course credits, GPA, and thesis

   thesis proposals and defense records). Students who fail to meet
   graduation criteria must reapply in the next semester. Institutes submit
   approved graduation lists to SR after verifying all requirements, ensuring
   alignment with academic regulations. Graduate students must also fulfill
   thesis-related obligations, monitored collaboratively with their advisors

🤖 RAG ANSWER (69.4s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Persons who find lost items on campus should report them by calling
   extension 3565 or delivering them to the Security Office in Building D2.
   Security officers prepare a two-copy Found Item Return Form, which is
   signed by both the finder and the officer. The original form remains at
   the Security Unit, while the second copy is given to the finder. Upon
   receipt, the Security Directorate office staff numbers the items and
   stores them, with valuables (e.g., electronics, money, precious metals)

   Sabancı University offers several research project fund types, including:
   1. **President’s Office Research Fund**: For full-time faculty who started
   at SU within the last two years and completed six months, with eligibility
   for those who previously received an Integration Project.  2. **Project

   Technology Transfer Office.  3. **SOP Fund (Industry-Focused Projects
   Program)**: Supports undergraduate courses (ENS 491/492) involving
   industry-related research projects led by fourth-year students and
   faculty.  4. **Personal Research Fund (PRF)**: Governed by specific usage
   principles to maintain SU’s research leadership, though details are
   outlined in separate instructions.  Additionally, postdoctoral researchers
   may access funds under EU projects, TÜBİTAK programs, or faculty
   assignments, as detailed in related procedures.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (51.7s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Sabancı University provides several types of shuttle services:  1.
   **University Shuttle Services** operate on routes and schedules determined
   by Transportation Services (TS), serving all university members (students,
   staff, etc.).  2. **Shift and Collection Services** cater to shift workers
   with arrivals and departures aligned to announced hours and routes.  3.
   **Private Student Shuttles** coordinate transportation between students
   and external companies.  4. **On-Campus Ring Service** ensures internal
   campus mobility via routes and times set by the University Health (UH).
   5. **Special Service Requests** fulfill shuttle needs for specific
   activities (academic, artistic, social, etc.) arranged by university units
   or faculties.  6. **Personnel Shuttles** support employees, interns, and
   subcontractors for workplace transportation.  7. **Temporary Vehicle
   Allocations** are available for short-term duties of university personnel.
   Additionally, guests without SUCards can access services by contacting TS
   and the Contractor, while shuttle route/schedule changes are managed
   through SUTicket under the 'Transportation/Ulaşım' category.
------------------------------------------------------------

   study or rest is forbidden, as is preventing other residents or staff from
   entering/exiting rooms or offices freely. Room assignments and changes
   must follow Dormitory Management guidelines, and students must use only
   their designated bunk, wardrobe, and cabinet without unauthorized
   alterations. Dormitories open 2 days before each academic term and must be
   vacated within 2 days after its end; students late twice without a valid
   excuse will be excluded from future stays. Additionally, misuse of
   university ID cards (e.g., allowing others to use them) is prohibited, and
   disciplinary actions may be taken for rule violations.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (66.5s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Special event and catering services are arranged by submitting the
   "Special Event and Catering Request Form" on SUForm **30 days before the
   event**. The Food and Beverage Services (FBS) prepares preliminary offers,
   including menus and specifications, based on the request. Units must
   approve these details and initiate a procurement request (PR), after which
   the Procurement and Operations Unit (POP) selects a suitable company and
   communicates the decision to the unit and FBS. Coordination with the
   selected company (e.g., Hilton ParkSA, MüzedeChanga, or Eurest) for
   operational details is conducted through FBS. Late form submissions may
   lead to disruptions, and the requesting unit is accountable for such
   deficiencies. Event-specific menus and pricing are provided by the
   selected company under agreements with the university.
------------------------------------------------------------

   tehlikeye atma ve sollama yasaktır.  2. **Hız limiti 30 km/saattir** ve
   sürücüler Güvenlik Müdürlüğünün uyarılarına ve trafik işaretlerine uymak
   mecburidir.  3. **Alkollü araç kullanımı kesinlikle yasaktır**; bu durumda
   araç kampüse alınmaz.  4. **Sürücü belgesi olmayan veya plakasız araçlar
   kampüse girişe izin verilmez**.  5. **Kask takmayan motosiklet/bisiklet
   sürücüleri kampus yollarında seyretmez**.  6. **Yasak yerlere bırakılan
   araçlar Güvenlik Müdürlüğünce çekilebilir**.  7. **Kural ihlalleri

🤖 RAG ANSWER (75.9s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Sabancı University's campus health services include emergency care via a
   24/7 ambulance team (doctor, anesthesiologist, driver) and diagnostic
   testing (blood work, X-rays, MMR/Hepatitis screenings). Students
   (excluding Exchange and Project Management undergraduates) receive health
   insurance coverage from enrollment until graduation. Mental health
   services are available through Psychiatry and Psychologist appointments
   arranged via email or phone. Prescriptions for chronic conditions can be
   refilled by email request, and approved referrals allow access to private
   contracted institutions. Preventive services include mandatory enrollment
   screenings (CBC, Hepatitis A/B, MMR) and onboarding medical exams for
   employees. Foreign students have a 40% participation rate in insurance,
   while employees’ coverage depends on HR contract types. Emergency
   protocols include ambulance transport to designated pandemic hospitals and
   quarantine management for disease contacts.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (98.6s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The Rectorate Research Fund (SU President’s Office Research Fund) requires
   applicants to meet the following conditions:  1. **Eligibility**: Full-
   time faculty members who have been employed at Sabancı University for at
   least six months and within the last two years. Those who previously
   received an Integration Project are also eligible, but faculty can apply
   only once.  2. **Project Outcomes**: By the project’s end, applicants must
   submit at least two new documented external grant applications (e.g., ERC,
   Horizon Europe, TÜBİTAK) or secure one guaranteed external financing. They
   must also publish high-impact scientific articles.  3. **Budget and
   Duration**: Theoretical research budgets are capped at 300,000 TL, while
   experimental research (defined as projects where >30% of the budget covers
   lab equipment, consumables, or field studies) is limited to 600,000 TL.
   The maximum project duration is 36 months.  4. **Budget Prioritization**:
   Infrastructure investments (e.g., devices, consumables, outsourcing) must
   be prioritized over personnel salaries (e.g., doctoral students,
   postdocs).  5. **Application Compatibility**: Proposed projects must align
   with the applicant’s existing or ongoing research activities.
   Applications are due by the end of May each year.
